    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        if (window.location.search.startsWith('?login')) {

<script>
    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        const passwordInputTrigger = document.querySelector('#password_input_trigger');
        const passwordInput = document.querySelector('#password_input');

        passwordInputTrigger.onclick = () => {
            if (passwordInput.type === 'password') {
                passwordInput.type = 'text';

                return;
            }

            passwordInput.type = 'password';
        };
    });
</script>

                        <h1>SLOT88 ! Portal Resmi Slot Demo Depo 5K Pulsa Gacor Gampang Maxwin Hari Ini Rekomendasi SIBAYAKTOTO</h1>
                        <p><a href="https://jpardo.edu.pe/">Slot88</a> hadir sebagai portal resmi yang memberikan akses mudah bagi pemain yang ingin mencoba<a href="https://jpardo.edu.pe/">slot demo</a> maupun bermain dengan deposit kecil hanya 5K menggunakan pulsa. Di sini kamu bisa merasakan sensasi permainan tanpa harus mengeluarkan modal besar di awal, sekaligus menikmati berbagai game yang sedang gacor dan memiliki peluang maxwin lebih tinggi. Platform ini dirancang sederhana, cepat, dan ramah bagi pemula maupun pemain berpengalaman yang mencari kenyamanan bermain setiap hari.</p>

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {

  const faqContainer = document.getElementById("faqAuto");

  // Ambil semua schema FAQPage
  const schemas = document.querySelectorAll('script[type="application/ld+json"]');

  let faqData = [];

  schemas.forEach(schema => {
    try {
      const json = JSON.parse(schema.textContent);

      // Jika array
      if(Array.isArray(json)){
        json.forEach(item => {
          if(item["@type"] === "FAQPage"){
            faqData = faqData.concat(item.mainEntity || []);
          }
        });
      }

      // Jika object
      else if(json["@type"] === "FAQPage"){
        faqData = faqData.concat(json.mainEntity || []);
      }

    } catch(e){}
  });

  // Render FAQ otomatis
  faqData.forEach((faq, index) => {

    const question = faq.name || "Pertanyaan";
    const answer = faq.acceptedAnswer?.text || "";

    const item = document.createElement("div");
    item.className = "faq-item";

    item.innerHTML = `
      <button class="faq-question">
        <span>${question}</span>
        <span class="faq-icon">+</span>
      </button>

      <div class="faq-answer">
        <div class="faq-answer-content">
          ${answer}
        </div>
      </div>
    `;

    faqContainer.appendChild(item);
  });

  // Accordion
  document.querySelectorAll(".faq-question").forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener("click", function(){

      const item = this.parentElement;

      item.classList.toggle("active");

    });
  });

});
</script>
<script>
document.querySelectorAll('.faq-question').forEach((question) => {
  question.addEventListener('click', () => {

    const answer = question.nextElementSibling;
    const icon = question.querySelector('.faq-icon');

    const isOpen =
      question.getAttribute('aria-expanded') === 'true';

    question.setAttribute(
      'aria-expanded',
      String(!isOpen)
    );

    answer.classList.toggle('show', !isOpen);

    icon.textContent = isOpen ? '+' : '−';
  });
});
</script>

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {

  const reviewContainer = document.getElementById("reviewAuto");

  const schemas = document.querySelectorAll('script[type="application/ld+json"]');

  let reviews = [];

  schemas.forEach(schema => {

    try {

      const json = JSON.parse(schema.textContent);

      // Jika array schema
      if(Array.isArray(json)){

        json.forEach(item => {

          // Review langsung
          if(item["@type"] === "Review"){
            reviews.push(item);
          }

          // Aggregate Product review
          if(item.review){
            if(Array.isArray(item.review)){
              reviews = reviews.concat(item.review);
            } else {
              reviews.push(item.review);
            }
          }

        });

      }

      // Jika object
      else {

        if(json["@type"] === "Review"){
          reviews.push(json);
        }

        if(json.review){

          if(Array.isArray(json.review)){
            reviews = reviews.concat(json.review);
          } else {
            reviews.push(json.review);
          }

        }

      }

    } catch(e){}

  });

  // Ambil hanya 2 review
  reviews.slice(0,2).forEach((review,index) => {

    const author = review.author?.name || "User";
    const text = review.reviewBody || "Review tidak tersedia.";
    const rating = parseInt(review.reviewRating?.ratingValue || 5);
    const date = review.datePublished || "";

    const stars = "★".repeat(rating);

    const initials = author
      .split(" ")
      .map(word => word[0])
      .join("")
      .substring(0,2);

    const card = document.createElement("div");

    card.className = "review-card";

    card.innerHTML = `
      <div class="review-top">

        <div class="review-user">
          <div class="review-avatar">
            ${initials}
          </div>

          <div>
            <div class="review-name">${author}</div>
            <div class="review-date">${date}</div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="review-rating">${stars}</div>

      </div>

      <div class="review-text">
        ${text}
      </div>
    `;

    reviewContainer.appendChild(card);

  });

});
</script>
